Nevertheless, there is a long tail of input sequences where the LLM and ENTD differ significantly, and we examine several possible sources of this discrepancy across the transformer architecture, training procedure, and finite-sample noise in the ENTD estimate itself. More broadly, we hope our findings will encourage more work on ``data-centric mechanistic interpretability,'' a complement to standard mechanistic interpretability that opens the black box of how model behaviors arise from the data, rather than how they are encoded in the learned weights.

</details>


### [156] [Towards a Unified Multidimensional Explainability Metric: Evaluating Trustworthiness in AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14315)
*Georgios Makridis,Georgios Fatouros,Athanasios Kiourtis,Dimitrios Kotios,Vasileios Koukos,Dimosthenis Kyriazis,Jonh Soldatos*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出一个综合框架，通过保真度、简单性和稳定性三个关键方面评估XAI方法的可解释性，并构建离线知识库以支持上下文相关的可解释性评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法缺乏统一的多维可解释性评估标准，且可解释性会因数据集、模型和用户领域知识而变化。

Method: 基于LIME和SHAP等方法，在多个数据集和模型上进行基准实验，评估保真度、简单性和稳定性，构建离线知识库并利用元数据估计未知场景的可解释性分数。

Result: 通过三个开源数据集验证框架，并讨论了结果与数据集特征的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为比较和评估XAI方法提供了稳健工具，有助于开发更透明可信的AI系统。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a comprehensive framework for assessing the explainability of various XAI methods, such as LIME and SHAP, across multiple datasets and machine learning models, with the ultimate goal of creating a unified multidimensional explainability score. Our methodology focuses on three key aspects of explainability: fidelity, simplicity, and stability. We leverage benchmarking experiments to systematically evaluate these aspects and use the insights gained to construct an offline knowledge base. This knowledge base captures the explainability scores for each registered model and serves as a valuable resource for context-dependent evaluation of explainability. By analyzing the complementary characteristics and metadata of AI models, datasets, and XAI methods, the knowledge base will enable the estimation of explainability scores for previously unseen datasets and models. Properties like fidelity, simplicity, and stability may vary significantly based on the dataset, underlying model, and domain expertise of the end user. We demonstrate our framework by applying it to three open-source datasets, discussing the implications of the obtained results in relation to the characteristics of the datasets. Our work contributes to the growing field of XAI by providing a robust and versatile tool for evaluating and comparing the explainability of various XAI methods, ultimately supporting the development of more transparent and trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [157] [Dysco: Dynamic Subspace Boosting to Mitigate LoRA Interference in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14367)
*Haobo Zhang,Jiankun Wang,Suraj Rajendran,Weishen Pan,Lam Tsoi,Yong Chen,Fei Wang,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出动态子空间提升方法Dysco，通过分配客户端特定LoRA子空间解决联邦微调中的数据-参数干扰问题，在合成任务和MIMIC-IV临床文本分类上显著降低损失并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调中异构客户端导致LoRA适配器聚合不稳定，现有方法未考虑数据-参数干扰的几何根源，即LoRA更新子空间与客户端激活之间的对齐问题。

Method: Dysco通过客户端计算激活不敏感子空间并传输基向量，服务端以闭式解构建客户端特定合并子空间，并通过多轮子空间提升保留历史更新方向并适应表示漂移。

Result: 合成任务中最终轮训练损失降低多达9倍，MIMIC-IV分类上五种联邦算法提升最高4.3%，优于近期联邦LoRA方法，仅增加0.9%额外时间开销。

Conclusion: Dysco有效缓解数据-参数干扰，提供更紧的聚合误差上界，是一种轻量且兼容的联邦LoRA优化方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large pre-trained models increasingly relies on Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to reduce communication and computation, but heterogeneous clients can make adapter aggregation unstable. We identify the data-parameter interference as a geometric source of this instability. This interference is controlled by the alignment between LoRA update subspaces and client activations, suggesting that federated LoRA aggregation should be viewed not only as parameter averaging but also as subspace allocation. We propose Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco), a plug-in method that allocates client-specific LoRA subspaces in a federated and dynamic manner. In each round, clients compute activation-insensitive subspaces from local representations and transmit only the resulting bases; the server then constructs client-specific merged subspaces through a closed-form solution that maximizes compatibility with other clients' insensitive directions. To handle representation drift, Dysco performs multi-round subspace boosting to preserve past update directions while adapting to future representations. We provide a convergence analysis that embeds the data-parameter interference as an aggregation-error term in a standard federated optimization bound, and prove that Dysco's server-fixed merged subspaces yield a tighter upper bound on this error. Experiments on controlled synthetic federated tasks and on MIMIC-IV clinical-note classification with Llama-3.2-1B show that Dysco substantially reduces interference, reduces the final-round synthetic training loss by up to 9 times relative to baselines under the orthogonal-subspace partition the theory identifies, improves all five tested FL algorithms by up to 4.3% on MIMIC, outperforms recent federated LoRA methods, and adds only 0.9% wall-clock overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/illidanlab/Dysco.

</details>


### [158] [A Noise-Robust Elicit-to-Optimize Framework for Distortion Riskmetrics via Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14373)
*Yang Liu,Yuhao Liu,Yunran Wei*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一个结合逆强化学习和强化学习的噪声鲁棒框架，用于推断代理人风险偏好并在扭曲风险度量下优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在代理人决策存在噪声时准确推断其潜在风险目标，且缺乏统一优化扭曲风险度量目标的算法。

Method: 通过自适应贝叶斯逆强化学习从噪声观测中推断风险目标，并扩展PPO算法加入分位数网络以优化条件扭曲风险度量。

Result: 理论上证明了有限区分性问题的存在性和算法指数级收敛速度，实证中验证了框架在复杂金融环境下的准确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为噪声环境下风险偏好推断和策略优化提供了统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a noise-robust elicit-to-optimize framework that integrates inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and reinforcement learning (RL) for eliciting agents' risk preferences and optimizing policies under a broad class of risk objectives characterized by distortion riskmetrics. On the elicitation side, we propose an adaptive Bayesian IRL method that infers agents' latent risk objectives from their noisy observed decisions, explicitly allowing agents to take stochastic and suboptimal actions. We establish the existence of a finite set of distinguishing questions that identifies the preferred distortion riskmetric within the candidate class and prove that the convergence rate of the algorithm is of order $O(\exp(-cm+O(\sqrt{m\log m})))$ under general settings, where $c>0$ is a constant and $m$ denotes the number of algorithm iterations. On the optimization side, we develop a model-free RL algorithm for optimizing policies under conditional distortion riskmetrics. By representing the objective as an integral of the conditional cost quantile function with respect to the distortion function, the method unifies distortion-riskmetric objectives. We optimize diverse risk objectives by extending the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm with policy, value, and quantile neural networks, where the quantile network estimates the full conditional cost quantile function and enables numerical evaluation of general risk objectives. A comprehensive empirical study demonstrates the framework's elicitation accuracy and effectiveness in complex financial environments.

</details>


### [159] [Reward-Free Evolving Agents via Pairwise Validator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14408)
*Minghao Liu,Yu Wang,Jiayun Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出用成对验证器替代标量奖励，作为自进化智能体循环中的接受/拒绝信号。成对验证器利用对比判断的稳定性，无需训练或标量校准，在多个引擎和任务中达到或超过基线性能，且节省了标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计可靠标量奖励是自进化智能体循环中的昂贵环节，需要领域专家和标注数据，且标量校准困难。

Method: 在多个自进化引擎中集成一个冻结的LLM作为成对验证器，替换原有的标量奖励门控，并引入自适应焦点和软Elo两种变体来指导父代选择。

Result: 在提示和代码两种基质上，该方法在多数设置中匹配或超过全奖励基线，且验证器互换后模式仍稳定。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A self-evolving agentic loop repeatedly proposes a tweaked version of an agent (its prompt template or program) and accepts or rejects the change based on a per-iteration quality signal. Designing that signal is often the costly part of the project: a reliable scalar reward requires domain expertise and labeled examples that are themselves as expensive to assemble as the agent's underlying task. We propose replacing the scalar at the accept/reject gate with a pairwise validator: a frozen LLM that, given the parent and child candidate, returns a binary verdict on which is better. Pairwise judgment is generally easier and more stable than absolute scoring, due to its contrastive nature, which mitigates the need for strict scale calibration. The validator also requires no training of its own. We integrate the validator into three published self-evolving engines (GEPA, ADRS, ShinkaEvolve) and report two flavors: Adaptive Focus, which retains the engine's existing val-set parent selection, and Soft Elo, which lets the validator's verdicts drive parent selection so that val-set rewards drop as well. Across multiple agents and two artifact substrates (prompt and code), our method matches or exceeds the full-reward baseline on the majority of settings we evaluate, and the pattern survives a cross-family validator swap. The pairwise gate is thus a drop-in replacement for per-step reward design at competitive task accuracy without the labeling cost.

</details>


### [160] [HyperShadow: A Benchmark for Detecting 3D Projections of Higher-Dimensional Spatial Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14419)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: HyperShadow是首个公开基准，用于检测3D点云是原生三维形状还是高维空间（R^4至R^6）中刚体投影的“阴影”，通过投影签名、密度折叠、径向剖面和拓扑变化等特征，190k参数网络达到96.6%准确率，并引入零参数刚性见证器实现0.982 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D标注仅指三维空间加时间，但缺乏检测高维空间投影（如阴影）的基准和算法，而该任务与固有维度估计本质不同，因此需要新方法。

Method: 利用投影签名、密度折叠、填充体积径向剖面和拓扑变化等特征，设计190k参数点网络；对时间序列引入Kabsch对齐残差作为零参数刚性见证器。

Result: 标准维度估计方法仅71-73%准确率，所提点网络达96.6%（跨4种污染等级），泛化至未见对象家族79-91%；刚性见证器AUROC 0.982。

Conclusion: HyperShadow提供了可控工具，通过可观测统计量判别数据是否兼容纯三维解释，不涉及物理现实主张。

Abstract: Machine-learning datasets labelled "4D" universally denote three spatial dimensions plus time. We introduce HyperShadow, the first public benchmark in which the fourth, fifth, and sixth dimensions are spatial: the task is to decide whether a 3D point cloud is a native three-dimensional shape or the projection, the "shadow", of a rigid object living in R^N (N = 4-6). We show this task is fundamentally distinct from intrinsic-dimension estimation: a shadow is still at-most-3-dimensional data, and standard estimators (TwoNN, Levina-Bickel MLE) reach only 71-73% accuracy. Detection instead requires projection signatures, density folds, filled volumes with characteristic radial profiles, and topology changes, which a 190k-parameter point network recovers at 96.6% accuracy across four corruption tiers, generalizing at 79-91% to object families never seen in training. On a temporal track of rigidly rotating objects we introduce a zero-parameter rigidity witness: the residual of the optimal rigid 3D alignment (Kabsch) between consecutive frames, which must vanish for any rigid 3D motion but cannot vanish for the shadow of a rigid rotation in R^N. This single interpretable statistic separates the classes at AUROC 0.982. All data are generated reproducibly from seeds; the dataset, models, and code are released publicly. HyperShadow makes no claim about physical reality; it is a controlled instrument for studying which observable statistics can certify incompatibility with a purely three-dimensional explanation.

</details>


### [161] [Adaptive Runge-Kutta Step Control Buys Training Loss, Not Generalization: An Honest Compute-Matched Study of RK-Adam Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14516)
*Akhilesh Gogikar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 使用高阶Runge-Kutta方法改进Adam优化器，在严格计算匹配协议下性能不如标准Adam，修复后虽大幅降低训练损失但泛化能力未提升，且梯度平均的隐式正则化效果可被低成本一阶方法替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释优化器为梯度流离散化的观点启发将高阶龙格-库塔积分器应用于神经网络，但缺乏严格计算预算下的公平比较。

Method: 构建Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK对的Adam变体，采用FSAL重用和局部误差步长控制，在计算匹配协议下与标准Adam对比，并通过消融实验分析机制。

Result: RK变体在训练损失上不如Adam；修复后的变体在全批量下训练损失降低约40倍，但测试精度无提升，且收益对初始步长敏感；梯度平均有隐式正则化效果，但被RMSprop和NAdam以更低成本超越。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Interpreting optimizers as gradient-flow discretizations has motivated applying higher-order Runge-Kutta (RK) integrators to neural networks. We build a representative Adam variant (Bogacki-Shampine 3(2) RK pair, FSAL reuse, local-error step control) and evaluate it under a strict compute-matched protocol giving every method the same gradient-evaluation budget - an accounting this literature rarely enforces. Under it the RK variant loses to plain Adam on training loss in both minibatch and full-batch (RK's best-case) training. Instrumenting it shows the "adaptivity" is illusory: normalized error stays far below tolerance, the step size pins at its growth cap from step one (98-100 percent of steps), and no rtol x hmax x h0 setting makes it act; tolerances spanning 100x give bit-identical trajectories. The method is exactly fixed-step Adam with an averaged gradient at 3-4x cost. Repairing it (true reject branch; error on the applied map) reverses the full-batch result - about 40x lower training loss than tuned Adam - and a fixed-step control isolates adaptivity (an emergent warmup-and-growth schedule) as the mechanism. But the gain is fragile to the initial step size and does not reach test accuracy. A pre-registered follow-up rules out the obvious explanations: deeper minimization does not overfit, and an explicit temperature knob only hurts - leaving a trajectory effect, the controller selecting a minimum generalizing 1.3-3.4 points below first-order descent at equal depth. An n=10 study confirms one secondary effect: gradient averaging is a genuine implicit regularizer, beating lr-matched Adam and AdamW on 10/10 seeds - yet RMSprop and NAdam match or beat it at a third the per-step cost. Higher-order adaptive integration buys deeper deterministic minimization and a small regularization effect, but nothing a cheaper, well-tuned first-order baseline does not already provide.

</details>


### [162] [WrAFT: a Modularized Automated Writing Evaluation System for Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14524)
*Adnan Labib,Yixuan Huang,Jiahui Wu,John Maurice Gayed,Zheng Yuan,Qiao Wang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出模块化AWE工具WrAFT，结合多种LLM与微调，在TOEFL作文评分与反馈上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AWE工具在评分准确性和反馈全面性上存在不足，尤其是深层反馈质量差。

Method: 将AWE拆分为评分、表层反馈和深层反馈三个模块，评估多种LLM并采用直接提示与监督微调，使用480篇TOEFL作文数据集。

Result: 评分QWK为0.84，RMSE为0.44；表层反馈认可率96.14%，深层宏观反馈93.03%，深层微观反馈94.69%。

Conclusion: WrAFT在评分和反馈上均表现优异，且提供免费公开的交互界面。

Abstract: This study presents WrAFT, a Writing Assessment and Feedback Tool, that delivers both accurate and reliable scores and effective comprehensive feedback to argumentative essays. WrAFT adopts a modular design by dividing automated writing evaluation (AWE) tasks into scoring, surface-level feedback, and deep-level feedback. In building the system, various Large Language Models (LLMs) have been evaluated, including LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct, GPT-4o, and Claude 3.7, through both direct prompting and supervised fine-tuning approaches. A proprietary dataset of 480 TOEFL Independent Writing essays with official benchmark scores was utilized. Benchmark-based evaluation shows that WrAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance in scoring, with a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.84 and a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.44 against official scores on a scale of 0-5. Human evaluation of system-generated feedback also reveals high approval ratings: 96.14 percent for surface-level feedback, 93.03 percent for deep-level macro feedback, and 94.69 percent for deep-level micro feedback. An interactive user interface has been developed for the system and is publicly available and free to use.

</details>


### [163] [Muse: Representation Geometry of Muon Beyond Normalized Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14536)
*Da Chang,Qiankun Shi,Lvgang Zhang,Di He,Yaoshuai Ma,Ganzhao Yuan,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Muon-style optimizers apply a polar map to matrix momentum, but their updates also depend on the representation of each parameter block before orthogonalization. We study this representation choice as a form of optimizer geometry and introduce {\method}, a family of Muon-style optimizers that shares the same momentum rule and Newton--Schulz backend across native, nearest-square, skinny, and vector representations. Each Frobenius-isometric representation induces a distinct polar steepest-descent geometry, in which the shorter matrix dimension determines the number of supported singular channels, the pullback scaling, and the constants in stochastic nonconvex convergence bounds. In a teacher--student model, curvature collapse and an isotropic Marchenko--Pastur spectral profile connect early-stage dissipation to the represented nuclear-to-squared-Frobenius norm ratio. Pretraining experiments on LLaMA2-130M and LLaMA2-600M, together with fixed-momentum diagnostics, show that balanced non-native representations can match the performance of the native representation, whereas reducing the shorter dimension weakens the scaling and singular-channel support, leading to behavior that increasingly resembles normalized momentum.

</details>


### [164] [Alipay-PIBench: A Realistic Payment Integration Benchmark for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14573)
*Shiyu Ying,Xuejie Cao,Yingfan Ma,Yuanhao Dong,Wenyu Chen,Bowen Song,Lin Zhu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了Alipay-PIBench基准，用于评估编码代理在支付宝支付集成任务上的表现，包含9个项目、18个任务实例和两种场景，通过技能辅助平均提升10.31个百分点的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支付集成是复杂的仓库级软件任务，现有基准缺乏对真实支付集成场景的评估，需要构建可控的测试环境来诊断模型能力。

Method: 设计包含基础功能补全和高级风险感知强化两种场景的基准，每个实例配有多层次检查（静态、单元、集成、端到端）和LLM辅助语义评估，采用技能条件对比评估。

Result: 六种编码代理模型在有技能条件下平均通过率范围为68.58%至91.37%，技能辅助平均提升10.31个百分点，提升幅度因模型、产品和场景而异。

Conclusion: Alipay-PIBench为评估支付集成中的模型能力和结构化指导提供了受控基准，有助于诊断模型在源码补全、可执行支付行为及支付领域需求上的表现。

Abstract: Payment integration is a demanding repository-level software task: agents must select a suitable product, implement coordinated client-server flows, verify payment outcomes, and preserve consistency between transaction and business states. We introduce Alipay-PIBench, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on realistic Alipay payment integration. It contains nine product-specific projects and 18 task instances, each organized into Basic functional-completion and Advanced risk-aware hardening scenarios. Scenario-specific rubrics support deterministic static, unit, integration, and end-to-end checks, supplemented by LLM-assisted assessment for semantic requirements. We evaluate six coding-agent models and report rubric pass rate (RPR). Under the with-skill condition, mean RPR ranges from 68.58% to 91.37%. Access to the alipay-payment-integration skill improves mean RPR by 10.31 percentage points on average relative to the without-skill condition, with gains varying across models, products, and scenarios. Method-level results distinguish source-level completion, executable payment behavior, and payment-domain requirements. Alipay-PIBench provides a controlled setting for diagnosing model capability and evaluating structured guidance in payment integration.

</details>


### [165] [Accelerating A/B-Tests with Counterfactual Estimation: Reducing Variance through Policy Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14604)
*Olivier Jeunen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种利用策略重叠来加速在线对照实验的方法，通过Δ-离策略评估降低方差，在策略重叠时优于标准差异均值估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准A/B测试中，处理组和对照组采取相同行动时，结果只带来噪声而无信号，导致置信区间膨胀，效率低下。

Method: 将随机处理分配机制视为元策略，采用Δ-离策略评估方法，利用策略重叠区域获得平均处理效应的无偏估计。

Result: 理论证明该方法方差与策略间差异而非原始结果方差成比例，经验验证在推荐系统、信息检索和LLM界面中显著提升统计功效。

Conclusion: 该方法在策略重叠时严格优于标准差异均值估计器，有望大幅降低在线实验成本并提高评估效率。

Abstract: Online controlled experiments are the gold standard for hypothesis testing in online platforms. Notwithstanding their ubiquity, they are notoriously expensive to run, and issues of variance hamper statistical power in assessing treatment effects. While standard variance reduction techniques leverage model-based control variates to reduce outcome noise, they remain agnostic to potential structural relationships between competing policies.
  In this work, we identify a critical inefficiency in the standard A/B-testing protocol: when a treatment and control policy agree on an action, the resulting outcome contributes noise but no signal regarding the treatment effect -- unnecessarily inflating confidence intervals. We propose a novel experimental protocol that exploits this policy overlap to accelerate experimentation. The key insight is to frame the randomised treatment assignment mechanism as a meta-policy, and leverage $Δ$-Off-Policy Estimation methods to obtain unbiased estimates for average treatment effects. We prove analytically that our approach recovers standard A/B-testing practices in the general case, but that its variance scales with the divergence between policies rather than raw outcome variance. Hence, we dominate the standard Difference-in-Means estimator whenever policies have common support, and the improvement is strict whenever the overlap region contributes non-zero residual variance. Empirical results corroborate these theoretical insights -- holding promise for significant impact on the real-world evaluation of recommender systems, information retrieval pipelines, and large language model interfaces.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Entropy: Correctness-Aware Advantage Shaping via Contrastive Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14614)
*Weiwen Xu,Jia Liu,Hou Pong Chan,Long Li,Deng Cai,Min Chen,Hao Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出对比策略优化（CPO），利用参考生成与普通生成分布间的对比分歧作为正确性信号，替代熵进行优势塑形，实验表明CPO显著优于基于熵的强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵无法区分有益不确定性与有害混淆，作为正确性信号效果有限，因此需要更可靠的令牌级正确性指标。

Method: CPO在令牌级别计算参考引导分布与普通生成分布间的对比分歧，用于优势塑形，并在理论和实验上证明该分歧可作为正确性指示。

Result: 在域内和域外基准上，CPO均显著优于基于熵的RLVR方法，且保持强泛化能力；正确与错误响应分别支持探索与利用，两者平衡可获得最佳性能。

Conclusion: CPO通过对比分歧更准确地指示正确性，解决了零优势问题，并优于熵基方法，为强化学习中的奖励塑形提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly uses entropy for advantage shaping. However, entropy cannot distinguish useful uncertainty from detrimental confusion, limiting its effectiveness as a correctness signal. We propose Contrastive Policy Optimization (CPO), which uses token-level contrastive disagreement between reference-guided and vanilla generation distributions for correctness-aware advantage shaping. Both theoretical and empirical results show that this disagreement reliably indicates token-level correctness. We further show that On-policy Distillation is a special case of CPO, where the posterior distribution is instantiated by an external teacher model. CPO also resolves the zero-advantage problem. Experiments on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that CPO substantially outperforms entropy-based RLVR methods while maintaining strong generalization. Further analysis shows that correct and incorrect responses naturally support exploration and exploitation respectively, and balancing both leads to the best performance.

</details>


### [167] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出ChronoQG框架，用于从时态知识图谱生成忠实于时间约束的问题，并构建了4个基准数据集，揭示现有方法难以保持时间约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQG基准基于静态知识图谱，无法评估问题是否忠实于时间有效性、事件顺序和答案决定的时间约束。

Method: ChronoQG框架集成时间约束分类体系、拓扑-时间子图采样和痕迹引导的问题生成。

Result: 在4个时态知识图谱上生成16,011个验证问题，评估表明现有方法在多约束和复杂时间类型下表现不足。

Conclusion: 静态KGQG与时态KGQG存在明显差距，ChronoQG可作为时态忠实问题生成的挑战性测试床。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

</details>


### [168] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出APAL损失函数，通过不对称惩罚和峰值区域加权，显著提升时间序列预测中极端峰值的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对称损失函数和聚合评估指标无法有效处理预测应用中低估高峰风险远高于高估的实际需求。

Method: 设计APAL：1) 对低估赋予更高惩罚；2) 增加预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重，并配套峰值关键评估指标。

Result: 在五个先进骨干模型和两个行人流量数据集上，APAL改善了尾部误差和峰值预测质量，但会稍微增加聚合误差。

Conclusion: APAL在峰值预测失败为主要风险的实际场景中，提供可控制的聚合误差权衡，是一种实用解决方案。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [169] [Proof-or-Stop: Don't Trust the Agent, Trust the Evidence -- Loop Engineering for Verifiable Evidence-Gated Lifecycle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14890)
*Jek Huang,Jeffery Hsia,Jiayi Sun,Freddie Shi,Wei Huang,Ian H. White*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种名为 Proof-or-Stop Lifecycle Control 的方法，要求生命周期状态转换必须基于可验证的证据，并对开源实现进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理的多步软件工作生命周期状态（如已审查、已测试等）缺乏当前证据支持，仅作为声明存在。

Method: 将代理输出视为声明而非生命周期状态，仅当有最新、可机械验证的证据满足相应门控时，才允许生命周期转换。证据基于信任模型，而非语义正确性。

Result: 无人值守循环通过10/10场景且零假DONE；本地密钥接收捆绑拒绝18种篡改类且零假接受；门控循环将可见通过/隐藏失败放大从31/1800降至2/1800；自用语料包含565个故事和1007个审查发现，94.8%已解决。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents increasingly execute multi-step software work, but lifecycle states such as reviewed, tested, DONE, and ready-to-merge remain claims unless supported by current evidence. We present Proof-or-Stop Lifecycle Control, a method that permits lifecycle transitions only when fresh, tracked-source-state-bound, mechanically verifiable evidence satisfies the relevant gate. The method treats agent outputs as claims rather than lifecycle state, and uses proof operationally to mean gate-admissible evidence under a stated trust model, not semantic program correctness.
  We evaluate an open-source implementation through mechanism tests, a powered control-policy ablation, and operated self-application evidence. The unattended-loop engine passed 10 of 10 scenarios with zero false-DONE, and local-key receipt bundles rejected 18 tamper classes with zero false accepts. In a 9,240-cell ablation, the pre-registered A4 versus A2-prime comparison reduced visible-pass/hidden-fail amplification from 31 of 1,800 injected cells under a compute-budgeted naive loop to 2 of 1,800 under the gated loop, a 1.6 percentage-point improvement in not-amplified rate with a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.8, 2.5]. A near-compute A3 versus A4 comparison, 14 of 1,800 versus 2 of 1,800, indicates that the gain is associated with enforcing review as a lifecycle gate rather than merely adding a reviewer. The self-application corpus contains 565 stories and 1,007 review findings, with 94.8 percent resolved, plus a 68-row high/critical cross-vendor exhibit. These results support Proof-or-Stop as a model-agnostic, host-neutral control layer for deciding which autonomous-agent claims a lifecycle may act on. The evaluation is limited to one model family, 24 ablation tasks, and a self-hosted corpus.

</details>


### [170] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文通过真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构划分，研究二元分类器的最优线性组合，建立了凸化经验风险最小化存在唯一性的充分条件，并针对三种分类器列出所有配置情况，推导出指数和对数损失下最优权重的解析公式，引入φ-前沿评估稳定性和数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二元分类器组合方法缺乏基于逻辑结构化的严格分析，且在高维情况下最优解可能不存在或非唯一，需要系统化的理论框架和显式求解方法。

Method: 利用真值表将数据集划分为等价类，通过多维推广分类校准函数凸化经验风险；分析不同分类器数量下最小点的存在性与唯一性条件；对指数和逻辑损失函数解析求解最优权重；引入φ-前沿概念。

Result: 给出了任意分类器列表下凸化风险全局最小点存在唯一的充分条件；枚举了三分类器所有解类型（唯一解、下确界、非唯一最小点）；导出了两种损失函数下最优权重的显式解析公式。

Conclusion: 该逻辑结构化方法为组合分类器提供了严格的理论基础，其解析解避免了迭代优化，φ-前沿有助于评估模型稳定性与数据质量，具有理论和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [171] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了CFM-Bench，一个统一的多域、多任务基准，用于公平比较信道基础模型（CFM）和任务专用模型，涵盖六种信道配置和六个任务组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFM评估在模型专用流水线中进行，使用不同数据、配置和指标，导致无法公平比较不同CFM或与任务专用模型对比。

Method: 构建了包含3GPP统计仿真、射线追踪、工业/航空测量和车载多模态仿真等六种信道配置的数据集；划分完全隔离的官方训练/测试集；禁止预训练使用基准分割；要求披露所有开发数据，并按物理层、无线接入网决策和通感一体化三大维度组织六个任务组。

Result: 基准提供了评估信道表示跨模型、域和任务迁移性的公共基础。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench填补了CFM公平比较的空白，支持跨任务和域的标准化评估。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [172] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 针对医学图像分类器公平性审计中的样本量问题，提出使用人口统计学条件合成生成器同时缓解训练偏差和检测评估偏差。在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务中，顺序预训练微调优于联合增强，合成评估器可准确重现真实子群排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少数子群测试集样本量过小导致置信区间宽于待检测偏差，传统公平性审计失效。

Method: 采用端到端微调Stable Diffusion 2.1生成人口统计学平衡的合成数据，分别用于训练（顺序预训练后微调）和评估（合成测试集替代小样本真实集）。

Result: 合成预训练实现~100倍真实数据效率超越全真实基线；合成评估器对MCC和Recall的Spearman ρ=1.00，准确重现真实子群排名。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [173] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出多轴最大K方法，通过组级强化学习目标提升扩散文本到图像模型的模态覆盖与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型生成的样本在视觉模式上覆盖不足，且对人物相关提示存在人口统计偏差。

Method: 定义目标模态覆盖问题，采用多轴最大K目标：对每组样本各目标类别取最大值后再求和，实现跨类别贡献的信用分配。

Result: 在合成数据与SD3.5-M上验证信用分配机制；在感知外观公平性评估中，保持图像质量与文本对齐，公平性分数提升0.23-0.36。

Conclusion: 多轴最大K有效提升扩散模型对预定义语义模态的覆盖，缓解偏差。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [174] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出多模态语义感知对比学习（MseaCL），利用放射学报告间的语义相似性减少假阴性样本影响，在儿童脑肿瘤分类任务中AUC提升至少22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对比学习假设批次内非配对样本均为负样本，但医疗数据中语义相似的样本被错误视为负样本，损害表征质量。

Method: 在3D脑MRI和放射学报告的儿童队列上，通过融合报告间的语义相似度作为软标签指导对比学习，减轻假阴性干扰。

Result: 作为预训练策略，下游儿童脑肿瘤分子分类AUC提升至少22.6%。

Conclusion: MseaCL可生成更鲁棒且语义对齐的多模态表示，具有临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [175] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种基于像素的世界模型在Atari Pong中的表现，发现所有模型在封闭循环推演和像素空间零样本MBRL中均存在显著缺陷，并提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）作为辅助像素重建损失来改善部分模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将世界模型作为基于模型的强化学习系统的一部分进行评估，很少单独研究世界模型本身的能力和缺陷。

Method: 复现五种世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）在Atari Pong上的训练流程，然后冻结模型并采用封闭循环推演诊断（使用独立策略与冻结模型交互）和像素空间零样本MBRL（在冻结模型内训练新策略并评估真实环境）两种方法进行评估，最后提出CGSReg辅助损失函数。

Result: 所有模型在封闭循环推演中均出现明显失败（如球消失、运动错误等）；像素空间零样本MBRL策略性能显著低于原始训练管线，DreamerV3的平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9；CGSReg改善了DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER的某些指标，但效果因模型和评估而异。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [176] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文以教程形式介绍SINDy方法及其扩展（弱形式、集成、约束、参数化），并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸管换热器两个工程案例展示其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程中许多现象的控制方程未知或部分已知，神经网络等代理模型需要大量数据且可解释性差，而SINDy通过稀疏回归从较小数据集恢复可解释控制方程，但其工程应用文档不足。

Method: 从标准SINDy算法出发，逐步扩展至噪声鲁棒的弱形式、集成、约束和参数化版本，并通过两例工程案例（无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸管换热器）进行实践演示。

Result: SINDy方法简单实现且足够灵活，在两个工程案例中成功辨识出系统动力学模型。

Conclusion: SINDy可作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具，兼具简单性和灵活性。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [177] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了协变量平衡诊断在离线强化学习应用于治疗推荐时检测隐藏混杂/模型误设的能力，发现现有研究存在高偏倚风险或诊断指标不足，因此其统计稳健性无法保证，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习研究在治疗推荐应用中可能存在隐藏混杂或模型误设，导致结果偏倚，而协变量平衡诊断未被充分用于评估这类研究。

Method: 通过协变量平衡诊断方法，检测离线强化学习研究中的隐藏混杂或模型误设问题。

Result: 结果表明，现有离线强化学习研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估这些研究，导致无法确认其统计稳健性。

Conclusion: 目前离线强化学习在治疗推荐中的应用缺乏方法学稳健性，未来需开发更有效的诊断工具和研究框架。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [178] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个范畴论框架LINCS，通过正切提升修复机器学习草图中的非组合性问题，并证明最终INC余代数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法通常依赖标量化或向量空间假设，缺乏对非组合性（图表不能通过商草图因式分解）的形式化刻画；该论文旨在用范畴论严格定义并修复此类结构失效。

Method: 以带交换条件、极限锥和余极限锥的草图形式化机器学习问题；定义基底缺陷为非组合性度量；通过正切函子提升模型得到正切学习草图；引入INC自函子迭代正切提升，利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终余代数存在。

Result: 理论上建立了基于范畴论的机器学习形式化框架，证明了非组合性修复的稳定不动点存在性。

Conclusion: LINCS框架为机器学习提供了严格的范畴论基础，能处理非组合性问题，实验验证在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习中进行中。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [179] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出端到端多智能体系统AutoSynthesis，自动完成元分析的各步骤，输出符合PRISMA指南的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成目前主要依赖人工，难以规模化。

Method: 多智能体系统，包含制定搜索策略、检索文献、筛选、提取统计量、计算效应量、随机效应元分析、异质性分析和偏倚风险评估。

Result: 筛查28+研究，提取20+定量声明，池化效应估计与专家元分析的Hedges' g高度一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能提高定量证据合成的可扩展性，支持跨学科循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [180] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（KP-树）实现实时嵌入更新，避免两阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段推荐中用户嵌入在两次重新训练之间固定不变，导致新评分无法即时反映，影响推荐效果。

Method: 使用KP-树（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好，拟合低秩投影，并根据评分实时计算嵌入。

Result: 在KuaiRec上，可变草图以1.8%数据读取达到0.810 RMSE（ALS为0.822），批量更新快8倍；新用户首评后<1ms获得个性化推荐；KP-树在稀疏数据上物品覆盖提升40-130%。

Conclusion: 可变草图有效解决了嵌入陈旧问题，兼顾效率与精度，尤其适合稀疏数据场景。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>
